# Nest progress

Updated 23 September 2026. The approved [product brief](native-rewrite/product-and-design.md), [architecture](native-rewrite/architecture-audit.md) and [implementation plan](native-rewrite/implementation-plan.md) remain authoritative. Historical implementation notes are preserved in Git history; this checklist describes current status.

**No milestone has met all exit criteria. No complete native vertical slice or device verification is claimed.** Foundations are merged and independently tested; integration is underway. All database execution so far uses disposable synthetic fixtures. No production data, migrations, purchases, deployments or releases have been performed.

## Current work

**Current branch: `codex/native-acceptance-continuation`. Main: `88d19cf`.** Today’s private financial approval list, its AI handoff and recovery fix are merged after clean Sol medium review and exact CI success. The migration rehearsal now applies 54 legacy and 192 native migrations to disposable synthetic data (one explicit `pg_net` exclusion; simulated Auth/Storage interfaces). Preservation, renewal conversion and partial shopping/retention cutover rollback are locally verified. Main includes the 191-native-migration rehearsal; the additional latest-summary migration is on the feature branch.

**Current candidate: saved daily summary discovery (`61594c1`).** The recipient-only database/API, native client, Today entry and assistant handoff are implemented and locally verified. Storage has clean review; the connected slice awaits review/CI. Native interaction and hosted scheduling/delivery remain unverified.

**Substantial work remains; this is not a nearly complete release.** Remaining code/verification includes complete legacy-writer cutover and recovery that preserves new financial history, a final action/acceptance audit, and fixes found by real native journeys. External gates include an approved isolated development backend, live AI configuration, Apple/APNs configuration, real iPhone/two-member testing, hosted scheduler/Storage verification and authorized existing-data rehearsal. No production data, deployment, scheduler activation or release has occurred.

**CI workflow credential blocker:** the reviewed core integration CI proposal is preserved locally on `codex/core-integration-ci` (`b7ca460`). GitHub rejected its push because the current OAuth login lacks `workflow` scope. Existing remote CI remains unchanged; real HTTP/PostgREST integration evidence is local until this proposal can be published. Independent implementation continues. Source merges still require clean exact-commit Sol review and passing required CI; production and release approval remain separate.

### Reminder storage — initial local verification

The gated migration draft adds strict canonical settings validation, household-readable reminder rows and inaccessible immutable operation receipts. Two disposable PostgreSQL tests pass for canonical recipient ordering, invalid timing/lead times/duplicate recipients, rejected mute-override authority, household isolation and denied direct writes/private receipt access. This draft does not yet expose authorized save/read/recovery commands or schedule notifications. Recipient membership validation belongs in the forthcoming save transaction. CI/review and security advisors remain outstanding for this draft; no hosted migration or push occurred.

### Renewal transport and session client — candidate

Authenticated list/detail/save/remove/recover/cancel routes now use the caller token and strict shared renewal contracts. The native client is connected to the authenticated session through Expo fetch. Receipts bind household, actor, operation and the complete original command; cancellation and recovery cannot substitute a different intent. This increment does not yet provide renewal screens or AI actions.

Three actual native-client → HTTP → PostgREST → disposable PostgreSQL integration cases pass: lost committed response with historical recovery/removal, foreign and stale requests plus cancellation fencing, and forged receipt ownership/intent rejection. Workspace typechecking and Oxlint pass (existing advisory warnings remain). CI and exact-commit Sol review are still required. No physical-device, hosted backend or production verification occurred.

### Renewal durable recovery — candidate

Account/household/lease-scoped SQLite now retains the exact renewal save or removal command before dispatch. The shared durable runtime recovers status after restart without automatically repeating writes, freezes unresolved commands, persists explicit cancellation and hides pending state after account replacement. The store refuses command replacement and cancellation reversal. A grouped store-composition helper preserves the configured function-length limit.

Four real SQLite/native runtime/HTTP/PostgREST/PostgreSQL journeys pass: committed response loss across restart, unresolved cancellation with late-send fencing, account replacement, and staging/cleanup failure. Workspace types, lint and formatting pass, including the final store composition. Renewal screens and AI actions remain unfinished. This is local integration evidence, not device verification. Transport commit `8fbaebb` has clean exact-commit Sol signoff; CI remains required. Recovery review/CI remain pending.

## Earlier delivery log

Earlier deliveries merged and pushed to main after exact-commit CI and clean GPT-5.6 Sol medium review:

- `c2c0314` — strict proposal/approval contracts; CI `35561147879`. Sol's missing saved-selection receipt binding was fixed before merge.
- `d086b29` — retained one-off recipe representation; CI `35561569589`. Fifty-two focused storage/API/cache/HTTP checks passed; no approval writer enabled.
- `e56281e` — bounded structured generation through the real AI SDK using simulated providers; CI `35562298604`. Twenty focused checks passed. Sol's library household-binding finding was fixed before merge.
- `e138420` — private proposal reservation/read/discard; CI `35562751912`. Eleven focused PostgreSQL/context checks passed locally and independently in review; security advisors found no issues.

The current worker increment grants one server claim per proposal, retains immutable completion results, and exposes owner-only deadline recovery. Completion rechecks current food constraints, household membership, cooking choices, saved-recipe content and the exact occupied-week baseline. It writes only private proposal state: no meals, groceries, preparation or financial records. Claim retries never authorize another model call. Interrupted work expires rather than being silently regenerated. A completion retry returns its historical receipt; current proposal reads remain separate.

Local evidence: 23 focused PostgreSQL cases pass, including ten new cases covering concurrent claim/completion, expiry, revocation, strict generated/saved content, mixed occupied slots, constraint changes, receipt rollback and ten completion/discard races. Security advisors report no issues; the ten new cases also pass after the final household-lock change. Merged as `666c752` after successful exact-head CI `35564138556` and clean Sol medium signoff (17 independently passing PostgreSQL cases). Local main was fast-forwarded and pushed; no PR was created. No hosted provider, production migration or device execution occurred.

The API candidate exposes authenticated generate/read/recover/discard routes with strict shared response binding. It reserves an immutable operation, reads fresh authorized context/week/busy availability and a bounded saved-recipe shortlist, then obtains the one-time worker claim before model dispatch. A changed fingerprint/week stops before generation. Retries recover stored state, cancellation leaves deadline-recoverable work, and neither missing credentials nor provider failures invent a successful preview. Read/recovery/discard work without model credentials. The optional planning secret is server-only, redacted and separate from caller authentication; no credentials were installed.

Twenty-four focused API/generation/actual HTTP–PostgREST–PostgreSQL cases pass. Nine new HTTP journeys cover concurrent model dispatch, both lost claim and lost completion acknowledgments, cancellation/late results, changed constraints before and during generation, familiar-only missing recipes, exact discard, current authorization and no business-record writes. Two new boundary cases reject mismatched worker/receipt/content responses. API/contracts typechecks, scoped lint/format and diff checks pass. Merged as `675eb53` after successful exact-head CI `35565014287` and clean Sol medium signoff (21 independently passing focused checks). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed without a PR. Provider outputs/Auth gateway translation are simulated; no live model, native UI, hosted deployment or device validation is claimed.

The native candidate adds a protected week-preview route reachable from Meals, a saved-recipes-only switch, virtualized proposal/ingredient views and explicit discard/recovery actions. It saves only account/lease-scoped request metadata in SQLite, never private recipe content or planning context. A dedicated authenticated reservation endpoint lets the phone persist the returned proposal ID before requesting any model work. Restart loads/reconciles the stored request without automatically generating. Exact discard confirmation and receipts prevent an interrupted attempt from acting on a different revision. Account changes hide the private preview and stale leases cannot dispatch generation after reservation.

Forty focused native/API/database checks pass, including eleven new client/runtime/SQLite cases and three native client → HTTP/API/AI SDK/PostgREST/PostgreSQL journeys. Coverage includes lost reservation/generation/discard responses, SQLite restart, current membership, storage failure before dispatch, changed local request identities and no meal/grocery writes. A virtual clock verifies generation's 180-second client bound while ordinary requests retain fifteen seconds. Mobile/API types, scoped lint/format and iOS export pass (`/tmp/nest-native-proposal-export`, bundle `3ae78d6cda7f2e5b7e9650969d0a19e9`); export is packaging evidence only. Merged as `9ac2609` after successful exact-head CI `35566631802` and clean Sol medium signoff (all 14 new native/SQLite/HTTP cases independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed without a PR. Native controls, back gestures, accessibility, hosted provider and physical iPhone execution remain unverified.

The approval storage candidate posts the exact ready proposal revision atomically into retained shared meal snapshots. It rechecks current owner membership, food/cooking/roster fingerprint, the whole week baseline and destination occupancy; expiry is checked after blocking writes. Immutable operation receipts survive later week edits, while a different approval or discard cannot reuse the same intent. Suggested recipes receive null library provenance and stable ingredient IDs without becoming saved recipes; saved recipes retain their reviewed body and library revision across later edits/archive. No groceries or preparation are created.

Forty-one focused PostgreSQL checks pass, including ten new approval cases covering every nonempty configured slot set, occupied-slot preservation, two-owner contention, approve/discard races, late expiry, food locks, strict inputs, private helper/receipt access and full receipt-failure rollback. The API candidate adds explicit authenticated `POST /v1/meals/proposal/approve`, accepting only operation ID, proposal ID and displayed revision. The native approval candidate adds an explicit confirmation explaining that the displayed meals become shared while groceries remain separate. Both approval and discard alerts capture proposal ID and revision, so an older alert cannot act on a different preview with the same revision. The phone saves the exact approval operation in account/lease-scoped SQLite before dispatch. Older metadata with no approval intent remains readable. Approval and discard intents cannot coexist; unknown outcomes block unrelated actions. Restart only recovers current state; explicit continuation retries the same approval only if no terminal outcome is known. A definite conflict clears only that approval and requires a refreshed explicit decision. Confirmed approval opens the selected saved week.

Forty-four focused native/cache/SQLite/API/PostgREST checks pass, including seven new native client/runtime/storage cases and two actual native approval journeys. Lost committed acknowledgment and SQLite restart recover without re-posting; partner week changes refuse approval, forged receipt mappings never fabricate success, and metadata/storage/access failures remain recoverable. Native alert interaction, navigation, VoiceOver and physical iPhone execution remain unverified. No live model, deployment or production migration was used.

The single-meal model candidate extracts the existing canonical choice binding and independent dietary check without changing full-week generation behavior. Internal replacement validates the owner/household/week envelope, ready/unexpired state and one still-empty target slot, then returns only that entry with its original ID/date/slot. The model sees the previous recipe and other meal titles as context, but receives only one output slot. Favorite selection retains an exact freshly authorized saved recipe and uses only the suitability check; it cannot rewrite the selected recipe or carry over another meal's calorie estimate. Same saved recipes or identical source content are rejected as replacements. No route, edit reservation, write transaction or UI is enabled by this internal model function.

Twenty-two focused simulated-provider/real-SDK/API/PostgREST checks pass, including eight new single-meal cases. They verify unchanged input/other entries, exact favorite retention, private projection, missing/stale/foreign evidence before spending, extra/wrong slots, unsafe/unknown checks, familiar-only behavior, cancellation and oversized review failure. API types and scoped lint/format pass. Merged as `175594e` after exact-commit CI `35569753701` and clean Sol medium review (22 independently passing checks). Remaining M5: finish checklist capacity/provenance integration and audit acceptance, plus live-provider and device acceptance of generation/approval. M6–M9 and earlier device/acceptance gates remain incomplete.

### Durable single-suggestion edits — storage candidate

The single-meal generator merged as `175594e` after successful exact-commit CI `35569753701` and clean GPT-5.6 Sol medium review (22 independently passing checks). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed under the owner’s standing branch-review workflow. No PR or automation was created.

The current edit-storage candidate reserves a strict owner-bound replace or saved-choice command and a fixed two-minute deadline. A proposal revision permits one pending edit, and an operation permits one server worker claim. Completion replaces only the target entry, preserving all other reviewed snapshots, and advances the private proposal revision once. Exact saved selections are captured and revalidated; stale food/cooking/roster/week state fails without rewriting the preview. Terminal results are immutable across later edits, approval or discard. Authorization precedes every historical retry. No active meals, groceries or preparation records are written.

Local verification passes 25 PostgreSQL cases covering the new storage behavior and existing approval regressions, including eight edit/approval-or-discard races, concurrent retry/claim, tenant and owner isolation, malformed commands, recipe substitution, familiar-only enforcement and atomic rollback. Three additional checks prove deadline expiry after a blocking week lock, in-flight partner preference changes and immutable replay after another edit/approval. Security advisors found no issues on the disposable fixture. API typechecking, scoped lint, full formatting and diff checks pass. Merged as `adea948` after successful exact-commit CI `35571099738` and clean Sol medium review (22 independently passing edit/approval cases). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed without a PR. The edit API, native controls, assistant actions and separate ingredient review remain unfinished. All database execution uses synthetic disposable fixtures; no production migration, live provider or device verification occurred.

### Authenticated suggestion edit API — candidate

Authenticated reserve, edit and recovery routes share the strict owner-bound command and durable storage. Reservation/recovery require no model credentials. Execution reads current authorized preferences, week and availability before acquiring the one-time worker claim; mismatched context stops before model spending. Saved choice reads the exact recipe directly, including recipes beyond the first fifty-entry shortlist, and performs only the suitability check. Replacement uses the bounded two-stage generator. Neither path approves the proposal or posts shared meals/groceries. Request cancellation leaves deadline-recoverable state; lost claim acknowledgment never starts a second provider run, and lost completion recovers the immutable result.

Thirty-two focused API/SDK/PostgREST cases pass, including eight new real HTTP/database edit journeys and three new adapter/worker forgery cases. Coverage includes exact-target retention, saved selection beyond fifty recipes, concurrent requests, lost claim/completion, unsafe/provider failures, owner/tenant/current membership, stale constraints before and during execution, cancellation and unchanged original preview on failure. API types, scoped lint, full formatting and diff checks pass. Merged as `8d990c3` after successful exact-commit CI `35571833763` and clean Sol medium review (all 32 checks independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed. Provider outputs remain simulated; native editing controls, assistant proposal tools, separate ingredient confirmation and physical-device/hosted-provider acceptance remain unfinished.

### Native suggestion editing — candidate

The private preview now offers Generate a replacement and a paginated saved-recipe picker for each entry. The action captures proposal ID, displayed revision and entry ID; a stale picker cannot target a newer preview. Account/lease-bound SQLite stores only the immutable edit command before dispatch, with approval/discard/edit intents mutually exclusive. Restart reads current proposal and edit status without sending a new model request. Unknown/pending outcomes block approval and other edits. Confirmed completion refreshes the canonical preview before clearing intent; failures preserve the original suggestion. An invalid or conflicting selection clears only after the API confirms that no operation exists, so incomplete/same-recipe choices do not trap the screen. Native HTTP uses expo/fetch and the existing bounded generation transport.

Thirty-four focused native client/runtime/SQLite/real HTTP checks pass, including nine new native cases and two new actual native → API → SDK → PostgREST → PostgreSQL journeys. Tests cover stale target capture, metadata-before-dispatch, lost completion/restart, unregistered intents, one-time suitability checks, immutable account leases, conflicting approvals, storage failure, invalid saved choice, stale library and current membership revocation. Mobile types, scoped lint, full formatting and diff checks pass. The final iOS export passes at `/tmp/nest-native-edit-final-export` (bundle `374d215486e0009f1da7694a9d18412b`), which is packaging evidence only. Merged as `ca7637b` after successful exact-commit CI `35572810680` and clean Sol medium review (all 34 checks independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed without a PR. Native controls, keyboard/navigation, large text and VoiceOver remain device-unverified. Provider responses are simulated. Assistant proposal actions and separate ingredient confirmation remain unfinished; M5 is not complete.

### Opening a proposal from an assistant handoff — storage/API candidate

The owner-only open command recovers the original generation receipt together with current proposal content, including subsequent edits or approval. This gives a native handoff its original durable request identity instead of inventing a new generation operation. Current membership and private proposal ownership are checked before historical receipt lookup. Expired unfinished generation recovers failure; opening never claims a model worker, posts a plan or needs model credentials. The authenticated API strictly binds receipt, current envelope and requested owner/proposal. An index restricts lookup to the unique original generation reservation.

Fourteen focused PostgreSQL/adapter/real HTTP checks pass, including three new storage cases, one response-forgery adapter case and one actual HTTP handoff journey. Existing generation recovery tests remain green. API types, scoped lint, full formatting/diff checks and disposable security advisors pass. Merged as `a7a5837` after successful exact-commit CI `35573412090` and clean Sol medium review (14 independently passing checks). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed without a PR. The native deep-link adoption and assistant generation/edit/discard tools still need implementation; this boundary alone does not finish that flow.

### Native proposal handoff — candidate

The private proposal route accepts a strict proposal ID and Monday week. It reads the original generation receipt and current preview without reserving or generating again. SQLite adopts only account-scoped request metadata after an atomic comparison with the previous operation; concurrent or unresolved generation/edit/approval/discard intents cannot be overwritten. An existing proposal keeps its saved intents. Wrong-week/owner responses, stale account leases and storage failures cannot reveal a newly opened preview. A blocked handoff remains available after the previous request is finished and reset.

Focused verification includes native client/SQLite restart, strict link parsing, original-operation adoption, concurrent approval protection, wrong-week/account rejection, lower-revision different previews and retryable storage failure. An actual native → HTTP → API → PostgREST → PostgreSQL journey opens an externally generated preview after a lost read and explicitly approves it with no additional model call. Mobile types, scoped lint/format and iOS export pass (`/tmp/nest-native-handoff-export`, bundle `6719dc2dbf14983e22e4430597f53208`); export is packaging evidence only. Merged as `bc52c21` after successful exact-commit CI `35574414221` and clean Sol medium review (all 27 focused checks independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed. Assistant proposal tools, separate ingredient review, hosted provider and physical iPhone acceptance remain unfinished.

### Private assistant proposal journal — storage candidate

The gated journal extension reserves generation, replacement and exact saved-recipe choices using server-owned operation IDs, and journals explicit discard. All four dispatch through the existing authorized native transactions. Generation and edit records remain immutable reservation facts after worker completion; current proposal/edit reads are separate. The model cannot inject operation, actor or approval fields, and plan approval is absent from the accepted vocabulary. Canonical conversation history replaces fabricated proposal writes with committed journal records. Native effects and receipts roll back if journal insertion fails.

Eleven PostgreSQL cases pass, including seven new proposal cases for concurrent replay, post-completion immutability, exact saved selection, owner/tenant/revocation checks, malformed authority, terminal conflicts and generation/edit/discard rollback. Four existing preparation-journal regressions remain green. Disposable security advisors found no issues; migration functions remain below the configured length limit. Merged as `7b732ce` after successful exact-commit CI `35574922152` and clean Sol medium review (all 11 checks independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed. This is storage preparation only: assistant tools, post-journal provider dispatch, bounded current-state reads and proposal cards still need implementation. No live provider, production execution or physical-device verification occurred.

### Assistant proposal tools and native approval handoff — candidate

Private assistant tools now generate a proposal, replace one suggestion, choose an exact saved recipe, discard, and read current proposal/edit state. Shared strict contracts reject model-supplied operations, identity and approval. Model work starts only after the authorized reservation is committed to the private journal; a failed/lost journal acknowledgment cannot dispatch a provider. Replay retains the original operation and one-time worker claim. Missing planning credentials reject new model work before journaling. Tool results retain the journal's reservation fact; fresh read tools establish completion or failure. Native cards open the exact private proposal/week, and approval is available only through the existing native confirmation.

Forty-one focused SDK, receipt-binding, native-card and actual PostgREST/HTTP checks pass. Seven new real integration journeys cover lost journal acknowledgment, lost worker completion, one-time exact saved choice, single-target replacement, private/revoked access, missing credentials, malformed authority, rollback and assistant generation → native card → HTTP preview → explicit approval. No shared meal is posted before that approval. Existing SDK cancellation/history and preparation-journal integration regressions pass. ### Separate ingredient review — contracts, domain and storage candidate

The [ingredient boundary audit](native-rewrite/ingredient-review-boundary.md) rejects legacy automatic materialization. New shared contracts address retained meal/ingredient identities, revision-bound pages, explicit quantities/units and per-source receipts. Pure selection reconciliation preserves exclusions and member edits; new/replaced or already-added sources stay unselected. No ingredient-name merging or unit conversion occurs. Grocery text codecs now count Unicode code points like PostgreSQL, allowing imported quantities/units to remain readable and editable without truncation.

Gated storage reads at most 100 retained ingredients per page and reports leftovers, missing recipes and empty ingredient lists separately. Addition locks the exact saved week, validates each source, atomically adds only selected rows and retains meal provenance. Household-wide source identity deduplicates concurrent additions even under different operations or actors. Existing groceries are never overwritten; receipts distinguish newly added and previously added sources and survive later checking/editing/removal or meal removal. Current membership is required for historical replay. Archived same-household categories become uncategorized; foreign categories fail. No proposal, shopping session or financial record is created.

Thirty-five focused property/contract/PostgreSQL/grocery-regression checks pass, including 1,000 generated reconciliation cases, complete 4,200-ingredient capacity, pagination, Unicode, quantity/unit preservation, pantry exclusion, same-operation replay, different concurrent quantities, generated one-off recipes, stale/foreign/leftover sources, full rollback and revoked replay. Disposable security advisors found no issues. API/mobile/contracts/domain types, scoped lint, full formatting and diff checks pass. Merged as `310855c` after successful exact-commit CI `35577909509` and clean Sol medium signoff (all 35 checks independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed. The native review/durable draft/confirmation, checklist provenance and assistant handoff still need implementation. This storage boundary is not a finished grocery-list flow; physical-device verification remains outstanding.

### Ingredient review HTTP API — candidate

Authenticated POST read/add routes share the ingredient contracts and caller-token database functions. Pages bind household, exact week revision and forward-only cursor; addition receipts bind actor, household, operation, baseline and every selected source in order. Unknown fields and fabricated/merged mappings fail closed. The add route accepts bounded full-week bodies up to 8 MiB; ordinary review reads retain the smaller request bound. No provider credentials are needed and no proposal approval is implicit.

Twenty focused contract, adapter, PostgreSQL and actual HTTP/PostgREST checks pass, including three new transport journeys: 200 sources across two pages with a batch exceeding the ordinary body bound, committed-response loss with immutable replay after a partner correction, and stale/revoked/foreign/malformed/oversized denial without writes. Four new adapter cases cover caller forwarding, UUID normalization, response binding and error distinctions. API typechecking and scoped lint pass; Effect advisory suggestions remain. Merged as `ba27524` after successful exact-commit CI `35578805010` and clean Sol medium signoff (all 20 checks independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed. Native selection persistence, confirmation/retry controls, checklist provenance and AI handoff remain next; no physical device, live provider, production migration or deployment was used.

### Native ingredient recovery — client and SQLite candidate

The account-bound native client uses Expo fetch through the existing authenticated session adapter and strictly binds review pages and addition receipts. SQLite stores only source identities, reviewed quantities/units, selections and exact confirmed commands, scoped by actor/household/week and current account lease. Compare-and-swap sequence checks protect newer exclusions from stale controllers. An uncertain request freezes draft replacement; acknowledgment validates every source and clears the selected flags atomically. Storage failure leaves the original request recoverable. This is online request recovery, with no offline queue entry or automatic replay.

Eight focused checks pass: three native client cases, four actual SQLite cases and one native client/SQLite → HTTP/API/PostgREST/PostgreSQL journey. Coverage includes lost committed acknowledgment followed by restart, unchanged partner corrections, strict identity/cursor/receipt binding, storage rollback, stale editors, duplicate sources, revoked membership, actor isolation and lease replacement. Mobile types and scoped lint pass. Merged as `ee46d61` after successful exact-commit CI `35579470006` and clean Sol medium signoff (all eight checks independently passed). Main was fast-forwarded and pushed. The native controller and screen still need connection; no native interaction/device verification is claimed.

### Native ingredient review — screen/controller candidate

A protected week-specific screen is reachable from Meals and an approved proposal. ### Local Calendar agenda — read boundary candidate

The separate local agenda adapter reads existing EventKit occurrences and explicitly projects personal title/location/notes for on-device display. ### Local agenda display selection — persistence candidate

A separate account/household/lease-scoped SQLite selection stores only the calendar IDs chosen for personal display. It neither grants sharing consent nor changes existing busy-sharing selections. Stale leases cannot read or overwrite another account's selection; malformed stored data fails explicitly. The native settings/runtime still need to use this store.

### Partner busy agenda — merged

The Calendar agenda now combines virtualized personal rows and distinctly labeled partner busy blocks. It consumes the existing authorized sanitized snapshot API and shared domain freshness rules. Whole-day and partial-day coverage are explicit; a lunchtime capture can show afternoon evidence while the morning remains unknown. Assistant preparation editing `1475bcf` is merged and pushed to main after successful exact-head CI `35559038642` and clean Sol medium signoff. Preparation creation and due-date changes now reuse the existing on-device Zurich-day calendar read before saving. Busy and unknown results offer an explicit save-anyway choice; text-only edits skip the calendar check. The warning identifies its scope as the current phone's selected calendars and does not infer partner availability or a precise timed conflict. Forms stay locked while a warning is open; dismissal, navigation, account-scope replacement or abort prevents a stale save callback. Current loaded/ready consent and selection must agree, and changes during the read reduce the result to unknown. No permission prompt, event upload, new consent or calendar write is introduced. Assistant preparation guidance uses the existing authorized fresh busy-interval tool for the due date and preserves nonblocking warnings.

Local verification: twenty-five calendar/preparation cases pass, including three new warning/consent cases, cancellation/late acceptance, 1,000 generated Zurich day boundaries, DST and existing native preparation retry tests. The next M5 boundary is a server-only planning-context projection; see the [privacy boundary](native-rewrite/meal-planning-boundary.md). It reads both members' dietary constraints/portions and shared cooking choices while including only the requesting member's calorie goal. Missing setup stays null. Invoker RPCs are denied to anonymous/authenticated clients and require the trusted server role plus current actor membership in a two-member household. The Effect reader independently authenticates the bearer and household before sending a separate redacted secret to its fixed endpoint, binds the decoded response and sanitizes failures. No public API route, chat tool or client export exposes raw context. A timezone-stable fingerprint covers profile changes and membership incarnation for later approval checks; proposal approval enforcement itself is not yet implemented.

Thirty focused database/API/real HTTP checks pass, including four new projection cases, two credential/validation cases and one actual internal-reader/PostgREST journey. Existing food/cooking RLS and five preparation HTTP journeys still pass. API types, scoped lint and disposable security advisors pass. The fixture models only Supabase's secret-key gateway translation; PostgreSQL/PostgREST and the application reader are real. No hosted key or model was used. Exact-head CI and Sol review are pending. Structured generation, private proposal lifecycle/approval and ingredient confirmation remain incomplete; a server-only development secret and approved Gateway configuration will be needed for live integration.

## Milestone checklist

- [ ] **M0 — Decisions and execution route.** Tooling, delivery ADR, action inventory, exact dependency pins and linked EAS app identity implemented. CI enforces formatting, lint, typechecking and focused tests. Native smoke procedure exists but has not run. Exit still needs an executable iPhone development build and recorded native evidence.
- [ ] **M1 — Quiet native interactions.** Four-tab development preview, generated artwork, sample chore/checklist interactions, meal replacement and calendar toggles implemented. Every preview uses identified fictional data; release guards prevent presenting it as working data. Owner review, large text, VoiceOver, Reduce Motion and real keyboard/navigation checks remain.
- [ ] **M2 — Authenticated offline/AI slice.** Bearer membership API, chore read/completion HTTP and shared AI tools, transactional receipts, SQLite journal, Effect/AI SDK boundary, durable approval records and owner-private transcript persistence implemented. Native Today → outbox → API integration is merged in PR #15. Cold-start identity recovery is merged in PR #23 and locally verified. Private native chat and SDK/server lifecycle are merged in #25–#27; atomic household command journaling is merged in #28. Mutating assistant tools and canonical native receipt links are merged in #29. Live provider/structured generation, approval-resume integration and an actual native retry/restart journey remain.
- [ ] **M3 — Identity, onboarding and settings.** Native Apple sign-in, SecureStore session adapter, refresh/membership verification and retryable logout merged in PR #14. Private food-profile storage and API are merged in #30–#31; the native form and exact save recovery are merged in #32. Journaled AI food settings are merged in #33; shared cooking storage/API is merged in #34; the native cooking form is merged in #35 and corresponding journaled AI actions are merged in #36. Private memory storage/API/native management/AI actions are merged in #37–#40. Calendar consent/API/native sharing/AI availability are merged in #41–#43. Notification preference storage/API/native controls are merged in #44–#45; corresponding AI actions are merged in #46. Independent quick-start/comprehensive setup and assistant handoff are merged in #47. Progressive prompts at real feature entry, notification OS permission/enrollment and actual onboarding device acceptance remain. Existing-member Apple linkage and Keychain behavior need device verification.
- [ ] **M4 — Today, chores and groceries.** Real authenticated Today chores, online routine creation/edit/pause/resume/archive/skip/reschedule and corresponding journaled AI actions are merged. Native handover request/recipient acceptance/decline, one-statement coherent reads and readonly offline cache are merged through #71; corresponding private AI handover tools are merged in #72. Groceries support online add/edit/remove, inline add, checked collapse and offline checks. Account-level reconnect and cold-start identity recovery are merged. Grocery grouping is merged in #74; queue/receipt retention bounds are merged in #73. Today now composes real meals, renewals, calendar, variable bills and private financial confirmations; the saved-summary discovery candidate is under review. Final aggregation usability and actual two-device fault/acceptance journeys remain. Node SQLite and real PostgreSQL/HTTP evidence do not establish device execution.
- [ ] **M5 — Meals and week planning.** Week board, manual placement/move/replacement/removal, saved-recipe CRUD/selection, leftovers and linked preparation have storage, authenticated API, native UI and private AI implementations. Generated private proposals and explicit exact-revision approval are merged across storage/API/native. Single-suggestion replacement and exact saved-choice generation, durable edits and API are merged; native controls are merged after CI and independent review. Assistant proposal actions and native preview handoff are merged after CI and independent review; separate ingredient review, pantry exclusion and idempotent grocery addition are also merged with API/native/AI coverage. Real provider compatibility/dietary suitability and the full seven-day journey on an iPhone remain unverified.
- [ ] **M6 — Calendar and availability.** Read-only Expo adapter and consent-bound sanitized server snapshots implemented separately. Consent incarnation invalidates delayed requests across membership removal/rejoin. Authorized API, native selection/consent/publisher and assistant availability reads are merged in #41–#43. Local selections and pending consent are account-leased; foreground/reconnect publishing uses a 31-day horizon and 15-minute expiry. Authorized busy snapshots now feed proposal generation and preparation overlap warnings. Personal agenda and partner busy UI are merged with local/controller/HTTP verification. Production Calendar route integration and optional chore/renewal layers are implemented and merged with local verification. Optional background refresh and two-device privacy/permission inspection remain; foreground refresh is the implemented behavior.
- [ ] **M7 — Money and recurring automation.** Balance/history/detail, equal/exact/percentage expenses, full/partial settlements, append-only corrections/refunds, receipt selection/upload/recovery/removal, private AI proposals/confirmation and durable financial recovery have local implementations merged after CI/review. Recurring configuration, pause/cancel/resume and their private AI approval flows are merged. Fixed-cycle transaction invariants and explicit variable-cycle entry/Save recovery are merged; variable private approvals and AI proposal handoff are merged after CI/review. Explicit manual linkage, bounded scheduler execution/checkpoints and native cycle history are merged. Legacy rule/draft inventory, direct dismissal and private AI dismissal approvals are merged. Retained draft confirmation and explicit native/AI adoption with old-writer fences are merged; full migration reconciliation remains. Hosted scheduler activation, full migration reconciliation, hosted Storage/provider validation and native device acceptance remain. No scheduled production posting is active.
- [ ] **M8 — Renewals, reminders and push.** Renewal CRUD and reminder editing, corresponding AI actions, all six reminder sources, deterministic summaries, bounded scheduling/checkpoints, push enrollment/recovery/logout handling, delivery tickets/receipts/invalid-token handling and protected notification links are implemented with local evidence. The latest saved-summary discovery is a connected candidate under review/CI. Hosted workers, real permission/token lifecycle, APNs delivery and cold-launch navigation on an iPhone remain unverified. No actual device enrollment/delivery is claimed.
- [ ] **M9 — Migration and release rehearsal.** The full legacy/native chain and representative synthetic reconciliation are locally verified with explicit infrastructure exclusions. Immutable renewal conversion provenance, retry/rollback and partial shopping/retention cutover are implemented; the latter preserve exact financial history after rehearsal rollback. Complete writer drainage/cutover, pending-command epoch handling, durable rollback preserving post-cutover financial events, authorized existing-data rehearsal, both-member usability acceptance and a release-mode native build remain. Production migration/cutover and TestFlight/App Store publication require separate owner approval.

## Exact external blockers and required owner/device inputs

1. **iPhone execution:** Linux has no Xcode/xcrun. EAS Simulator reported unavailable. PR #50 linked project `@drrius-dev/nest` (`aca37c4f-1bcd-4e9a-a695-37e794f97933`) with bundle `ch.drrius.nest`. The earlier quota check belonged to the previous project and does not establish availability for this identity. No build was started by this task. Need a signed development build installed on a test iPhone (or an available iOS runner); signing/device enrollment requires the owner's Apple access. No paid build is authorized.
2. **Live identity/backend:** Need an isolated development backend/API and Apple provider setup that preserves the existing two members' identities. Confirm App ID grouping/audience before connecting real accounts. Public configuration is documented in `apps/mobile/.env.example`; no server secrets belong in the app. No production database has been provisioned, read or migrated for these tests.
3. **Live AI:** Need an approved provider/key and budget configuration for the isolated API to verify streaming, structured proposals and approval-resume. No paid request or service purchase has been made for this integration.
4. **Device acceptance:** Eventually both members' iPhones, real selected calendars and permission states are required for privacy, background retry, secure-storage failure recovery, push delivery and usability/accessibility acceptance.
5. **Review service:** Greptile cannot review further while trial credits are exhausted. Independent fallback is authorized and functioning, using **GPT-5.6 Sol with medium reasoning** at the owner’s request; this does not block source progress. CI and clean exact-head review remain mandatory.
6. **Production/release:** Existing financial history and excluded legacy records stay intact. Full fixture reconciliation and a concrete cutover/release plan must precede separate approval. No merge grants permission to migrate, retire the web app or publish.

These gaps do not block independent implementation. Current work implements M5 recipes and planning, then continues through calendar, money, reminder and migration workflows.

## PR #15 review corrections

Independent review found that a later network failure could clear a prior authorization denial, and warm foreground revalidation unmounted the loaded chore screen offline. Denial now stays blocked until successful authorized work. Same-actor warm revalidation retains only the previously verified in-memory identity during transient failures; logout, actor changes and definitive denial clear it. Added direct verifier/subscription and controller regressions. Self-review also added an expected-household header checked against verified API membership plus household-bound response envelopes, preventing a same-actor household change from contaminating another cache. All corrections passed exact-head independent rereview and CI before merge.

Rereview also found transient credential refresh failures incorrectly mapped to session loss. The credential adapter now separates retryable refresh/storage availability from confirmed missing/rejected credentials. Two adapter-to-controller regressions prove warm offline enqueue and sticky confirmed loss; all 15 chore tests pass locally.

PR #21 merged after clean independent review of `cdfc06442db4cf1fe000c67ba17f6b7a4827a102`, both current-head CI runs and resolved conversations. **Merged #22:** the account provider owns both shared chore/grocery controllers independent of screen mounts. Pinned `expo-network@57.0.2` prompts authorized sync on foreground/reconnect; delayed initial readings cannot override newer events, duplicate online events are ignored, and disposed/background listeners cannot trigger retries. Manual/foreground retry remains available if native monitoring fails. Retained online grocery edits are never automatically sent. Review found a lost reconnect trigger when an earlier request failed afterward; the loop now honors queued retries after failure. A process-lifetime connectivity hub avoids cancelling the pinned iOS monitor across account subscriptions while dropping old account callbacks. Nine new event/SQLite/sync cases pass; all 72 affected tests pass locally. Types and the initial iOS export pass locally (`/tmp/nest-reconnect-export`). Actual iPhone reachability/background callbacks remain unverified. Exact-head independent rereview signed off on `649b68668c5ce59bf0278d740a9456cc45e709a8`; both CI runs passed, all conversations were resolved, and the source-only squash merge is `9b7de03`. Updated export: `/tmp/nest-reconnect-fixed-export`. Connectivity is only a hint, never proof of API reachability or authorization; see [Expo Network](https://docs.expo.dev/versions/latest/sdk/network/). Cold-start offline identity and queue retention remain separate gaps.

**Merged #23:** protected credentials and last verified household identity share one serialized Keychain record. The adapter preserves existing raw SDK sessions, stores no additional token copy, strips identity on actor replacement and removes both on SDK logout. Cold recovery handles expired-token network failures without interpreting the SDK's null INITIAL_SESSION as confirmed logout. A durable logout marker prevents recovery after interruption and rejects late token writes; explicit sign-in reopens persistence. Live commands still use current SDK credentials and server membership checks. Tests cover real pinned SDK transient/definitive refresh behavior, account/logout races, schema migration and SQLite restart isolation. All 101 affected session/offline/chore/grocery tests pass locally, including 29 session cases. Review caught malformed JSON blocking sign-in/logout recovery; invalid JSON now fails closed and permits explicit replacement. Review also caught the logout marker skipping remote revocation; a pre-logout token is now retained only for the best-effort revocation call, with online and unavailable-network SDK regressions. Self-audit fixed warm cached identity surviving a denial while protected cleanup was pending. Types/lint/format and iOS export (`/tmp/nest-cold-identity-export`) pass. Clean independent rereview of `76dc8e4be197b651b5330949b6a2d618dedc48e1` passed all 29 session tests; both exact-head CI runs passed and all conversations were resolved. Squash merge: `2244f23`. Actual Keychain/device execution remains unverified. A failed Keychain write cannot promise durable logout intent until retry succeeds; the current screen stays hidden and logout is not reported complete. Queue retention remains a separate gap.

**Merged #24:** additive private turn ownership claims each prompt atomically with the saved user message. Retries return the existing turn without another claim; one conversation cannot run competing turns. Finalization binds a server-assigned assistant ID and transcript revision, persists one completed/interrupted result, and preserves immutable replay after later work. A two-minute ownership deadline rejects late completion; expired/interrupted turns never automatically reacquire generation. Existing transcript saves cannot overwrite running history. Sixteen new PostgreSQL cases and ten existing conversation cases pass with the combined migrations; all 92 database cases pass. Supabase security advisors report no issues on the disposable fixture. API/SDK model execution, tool-invocation persistence, approval resume and native chat remain unimplemented; this is storage evidence, not a working AI stream. Independent review signed off on `43bac2d233fb30b9e87131151b304d2373b6e9ae` after independently passing all 26 focused cases. Both exact-head CI runs passed, review conversations were resolved, and squash merge is `da37186`. No production migration executed.

Server-only Gateway configuration has no default model and fails closed when absent. Streaming exposes the real authorized chore/grocery read tools only; mutating tools await invocation journaling. The server loads canonical history after claiming, validates supported SDK parts, bounds context/steps/output/time, masks provider failures and persists completed/interrupted responses. Explicit deadline recovery never starts a model. Regressions cover malformed known calls and unknown tools followed by a mutation; neither creates a journal/effect or another model step. Known uncertain writes remain fail-closed; never-executed unknown error parts are omitted from saved responses so they cannot poison future history. Nine mutation integration cases (including subtests) pass, and restored history validates. Updated-head CI and independent rereview remain required.

PR #33 adversarial review reproduced a Unicode contract mismatch: SQL accepted 61 astral characters (61 code points) while the shared JavaScript schema rejected them (122 UTF-16 units). This made the stored profile unreadable and recovered journal input invalid for the actual SDK. The additive migration now aligns text bounds and blank-only validation with the shared codec, including ECMAScript whitespace. Direct native/journal RPC regressions reject the invalid boundary without any effect; 60 astral characters remain readable and canonical SDK history validates. A generated 65-case Unicode/whitespace comparison proves SQL/shared-codec agreement. All nine food-journal database cases and five food SDK integration journeys pass. ### Retained Money history read — candidate

Gated `20260921104133_native_money_history_read.sql` returns bounded 50-event pages ordered by occurrence date, full PostgreSQL creation microseconds and UUID. A tenant-authorized event ID anchors keyset paging, so equal timestamps are not skipped and a foreign/missing cursor has the same unavailable result. ### Bounded recurring server runner — candidate

The server-only Effect adapter validates the backend origin and redacted secret, disables redirects, limits each request to ten seconds, strictly decodes scan/results and binds entire input/job identities. The runner processes at most 25 candidates sequentially, with bounded pages and deterministic SHA-256 UUIDv8 job IDs across restarts. It advances continuation past failed candidates, returns explicit outcomes, retains the last processed cursor on scan failure and resets only at the end of a sweep. A later sweep revisits uncommitted failed candidates. Exact committed-job recovery does not post twice. Continuation is returned to the caller, not yet durably persisted; no scheduled endpoint or hosted timer is connected.

Focused real PostgreSQL/PostgREST/HTTP tests cover bounded resume, deterministic exact-job replay, lost committed replies, failed candidate progress and next-sweep retry, retained cursor after scan failure, invalid budgets and substituted page/job responses. Initial fixture requests incorrectly used the application URL instead of PostgREST and counted a public table under private; both fixture errors were fixed. The runner now uses Web Crypto through Effect, avoiding unavailable Node-specific types in the API's portable runtime. Verification logs: `/tmp/nest-worker-runner-{tests,types,lint,format}.log`. Exact-commit Sol review and CI remain required.

### Durable recurring worker checkpoint — candidate

Runner `990f11b490a591dd5d8474f24cd3325f4a47560c` received clean exact-commit Sol medium signoff and independent 4/4 HTTP checks. CI `35672106792` remains pending.

A gated service-only checkpoint now serializes one ten-minute run lease, retains the starting cursor/budget/expiry, and atomically records the bounded completion summary and next cursor. Exact claim retries recover the active lease without extending it. Exact finish retries recover the stored acknowledgment even after another run starts. Expired/superseded workers cannot move progress; a new run resumes the last committed cursor, and unacknowledged financial jobs remain duplicate-safe through existing cycle/job receipts. A finished sweep resets discovery; a failed scan preserves progress. The checkpoint carries only run counts, cursor and finite failure categories, never server credentials.

The strict Effect checkpoint client binds run/budget/full summary, and server orchestration now claims, processes the bounded worker batch and durably finishes. There is still no authenticated hosted scheduling endpoint or timer registration. A stopped worker leaves its lease until expiry; scheduling recovery must tolerate that delay. An expired worker may finish an already-started job transaction, but cannot overwrite the newer checkpoint, and cycle uniqueness prevents duplicate financial posting.

Four actual PostgreSQL tests cover service/member/anonymous grants, concurrent claim contention, exact request/reply recovery, expired/superseded owners, malformed counts/cursors, failed-scan continuation and injected checkpoint-write rollback of completion. Six HTTP/PostgREST tests pass including the four prior runner checks and two new durable orchestration/restart/lost-finish/forged-response checks. An initially accepted cursor with an extra revision field was fixed through exact-key validation. The concurrency fixture was corrected to capture the expected losing SQL exception. Types/lint/format and database advisor evidence are recorded in `/tmp/nest-checkpoint-{types,lint,format,tests,http,advisors}.log`. Exact-commit Sol review and CI remain required.

### Bounded materialization draft — 23 September

Materialization now holds a per-window cursor lock, reads at most 250 reminder keys through the composite primary key, and checks each candidate by exact household/item identity. At most two configured recipients produce 500 writes per call. Cursor and outbox changes commit together; concurrent calls advance separate pages. A short page resets the cursor so later sweeps revisit newly inserted earlier keys and changed consent. Zero inserted rows does not establish sweep completion. Future scheduler activation must keep visiting the window and define retention/catch-up policy for scan rows.

Both focused PostgreSQL cases pass after this change; the 1,001-record case additionally proves that two concurrent calls advance exactly 500 source records. These tests do not yet instrument query-plan work, cover membership leave/rejoin, or establish a complete delivery lifecycle. Cancellation scanning still needs examination for the analogous sparse-obsolete-work case. Exact-commit review and CI remain required; no scheduling job is active.

### Session-fenced push logout — 23 September, candidate

An additive gated migration binds new registrations to the authenticated JWT session. Logout atomically disables that session's registrations and permanently fences further enrollment by that session, including late requests for previously absent or reassociated installations. Fresh sign-ins can enroll deliberately; replaying historical receipts never transfers session ownership. Repeated old logout cannot disable another actor or a newer session. Revocation does not require continued household membership. Existing unbound registrations are disabled when this migration is eventually applied and require explicit re-enrollment; no hosted migration has been run.

Four real PostgreSQL cases pass for account-switch races, logout-before-enrollment, removed membership, concurrent enrollment/logout, private ACLs, atomic rollback and historical replay ownership. The signed-JWT HTTP logout journey and all three affected enrollment/client/recovery journeys pass against PostgREST/PostgreSQL with the new migration. Three protected installation adapter cases pass. Scoped lint and disposable local security advisors pass. Native callback integration, expired/missing credential recovery, permission controls and physical delivery remain unfinished. Current Supabase session documentation confirms the JWT session claim; the changelog Markdown fetch remained unsupported.

Enrollment/retry `b6c884c596d742de198cf0c43dd0594935308dfa` passed exact CI `35795259177` and clean Sol review; main was fast-forwarded and pushed. The logout migration requires its own exact-commit review and CI before merge.

### Native logout transport — 23 September, candidate

The Effect client calls the session revocation RPC directly with the retained logout bearer and publishable key, without ordinary SDK hydration or membership lookup. It strictly decodes a token-free receipt and binds actor/session to the request token's claims; only the server verifies those claims for authorization. It disables redirects/cookies, applies a bounded timeout and scrubs transport/parser failures. The actual signed-JWT HTTP/PostgREST/PostgreSQL journey passes lost-acknowledgment retry, substituted actor/session, false revocation, excess token and malformed-credential rejection; three existing registration contract cases also pass. Mobile typechecking and scoped lint pass.

This client is not connected to the native sign-out callback yet. Recovery must preserve a way to finish revocation after the retained access token expires, and the current `beginSignIn` path must not discard unfinished cleanup. These are implementation gaps, not requests for owner approval. No device or hosted behavior is claimed.

### Connected logout and protected credential refresh — 23 September, candidate

Native sign-out now requires notification cleanup before deleting credentials. A protected cleanup-required flag blocks both SDK deletion and replacement sign-in; pending logout is restored as hidden UI during runtime initialization. A failed initial protected read can retry initialization. Devices without a protected installation skip enrollment cleanup without creating an installation. Otherwise, the retained token invokes session revocation. Expired access tokens are refreshed through a separate non-persisting SDK instance, checked against the original actor/session and persisted with compare-and-swap before revocation. The rotated token is then used for best-effort auth-session sign-out. Cleanup completion unlocks credential deletion only for the exact current token.

Twenty-three focused storage/sign-out/pinned-SDK cases pass, including refresh-before-revoke ordering, restart after failed revocation, wrong-user/wrong-session/stale replacement rejection, failed protected writes, SDK deletion refusal before cleanup and successful deletion afterward. The actual signed-JWT HTTP/PostgREST/PostgreSQL revocation journey passes after extracting the shared token-identity parser. Mobile typechecking and final scoped lint pass. Function-length findings were fixed by extracting credential-record parsing, serialized storage and logout persistence without changing limits. Runtime mounting, physical Keychain, permission interaction and device delivery remain unverified.

Remaining lifecycle gap: a definitively invalid refresh token or missing/corrupt retained credentials cannot yet complete registered-device cleanup; a verified reauthentication/revocation recovery path remains to implement. This is distinct from ordinary expired-access-token refresh, which is tested. Enrollment controls and delivery are still unfinished. No hosted migration, push, purchase or release occurred.

Earlier sign-out hook `30457fc13aad3fc8b6fc8e81c0240b0475fa36e5` passed CI `35795457767` and was merged to main. Logout fence `ffd22e1` and transport `ff6ee92` have separate clean Sol signoffs; fence CI `35796310502` passed, while transport CI was still running at the latest check.

A gated additive RPC lets a freshly authenticated user revoke a retained older push session for that same actor. Actor identity always comes from the verified JWT; a target session belonging to someone else cannot affect their registrations. Existing current-session revocation delegates to the same private helper. One real database case passes for other-user isolation, old-session fencing, newer-session preservation, removed membership, missing session identity, null target and private helper ACLs. ### Logout refresh review correction — 23 September

Sol found a valid P2 in lifecycle `5438ce5`: ordinary auto-refresh could race the separate cleanup SDK on a rotating refresh token. That commit remains unmerged. Logout now stops new refresh ticks after durable intent and before cleanup; foreground activation cannot restart refresh while the subscription is hidden. Cleanup uses the same SDK instance to coalesce an already-running refresh. The pinned SDK discards a response when logout changes its storage snapshot; after that request finishes, cleanup retries its parent refresh token once sequentially, using Supabase's documented parent-token reuse behavior. Same-session SDK rotations may persist only into the still-hidden cleanup-required envelope. Wrong actor/session, older expiry and post-removal writes are rejected; the cleanup CAS accepts an identical result already persisted by the SDK.

Thirty-six focused session/storage/logout cases pass, including an actual pinned-SDK in-flight refresh crossing durable logout, its discard and sequential retry, protected rotation, revocation, and final credential deletion. Mobile types and final scoped lint pass. This is SDK/fixture evidence, not live Supabase Auth or iPhone acceptance. The earlier separate non-persisting refresh design is superseded; Apple recovery may still use an isolated authentication instance. Recovery RPC `8d75f6a` has a separate clean Sol verdict but cannot merge until the parent finding is fixed and rereviewed.

### Push cancellation fence — storage candidate

An additive gated migration serializes cancellation with enrollment. It either returns the immutable already-committed receipt or records an immutable actor/household/operation fence that rejects every late save for that operation. Cancelling an unsaved command does not modify device registration; cancelling a committed command does not undo it. Private session-fenced enrollment remains inaccessible directly.

### Rotation review finding — cancellation suppression fixed in candidate

Sol identified a valid P2 in `7ef6c95` and `14830c6`: after cancelling an unresolved rotation, a retained token event or cold-start reconciliation could restage the same token under a fresh operation. Neither commit is merge-approved. The fix persists the cancelled account/installation/baseline-revision/token-digest checkpoint before clearing protected intent. Automatic rotation checks this checkpoint again inside the serialized staging operation, covering cancellation between the initial check and staging. Successful register receipts also update their checkpoint before clearing intent, so delayed checkpoint writes cannot overwrite later cancellation. Explicit future enrollment or a changed token/revision can proceed. Corrupt checkpoint state now fails closed rather than forgetting cancellation.

### Bounded delivery retries — candidate

The gated retry migration permits at most three attempts for one occurrence/installation, only after a confirmed rate-limit rejection, with 30/60-second backoff and renewed eligibility checks. Receipt-level rate-limit rejection is eligible too. Unknown, accepted and permanent failures cannot requeue. Immutable attempt/send/receipt history survives newer attempts; historical acknowledgments remain recoverable, and ticket identity stays unique across retries. A worker crash ages a sending row to unknown in indexed batches of at most 100, without resending; a later known ticket for that original attempt may still be persisted.

Ten PostgreSQL checks and local security advisors pass for bounds/backoff, permanent/unknown denial, fresh authorization, concurrent retry, historical replay, unique tickets, late outcome recovery and the explicit 100-row expiry bound. Scoped lint passes; exact-commit Sol review and CI remain required. Delivery outcome `4ac038b` has clean exact-commit Sol review with seven independently passing PostgreSQL cases; CI remains pending. Provider transport, receipt polling, worker activation and device verification remain incomplete.

### Bounded push receipt polling — locally verified

Private polling state schedules the first receipt check after fifteen minutes, uses bounded backoff, and closes after eight polls or twenty-four hours. Each sweep inspects at most one hundred indexed due rows and returns scan progress. Missing receipts become an unknown delivery outcome, never automatic resend permission; a later valid receipt can still settle it. Ticket recording and poll creation are atomic, and terminal receipts close polling without replay reopening it.

Six disposable PostgreSQL cases pass, covering timing, concurrent claims, attempt/deadline limits, late receipts, rollback, bounded sweep progress and private access. Scoped lint passes with existing Effect advisory warnings; local security advisors report no issues. Exact-commit Sol review and CI remain required. This is private database scheduling only: no Expo HTTP call, hosted migration, worker activation or physical-device verification occurred.
